                                                      Corporate Introduction
                                                            公司簡介

Established in 1993, Kingdee International Software Group Company Limited (“Kingdee International” or “Kingdee”) is listed on the Main Board of the Hong
Kong Stock Exchange (stock code: 0268.HK) and headquartered in Shenzhen, China, and has become a leader in the international market and the best
player in China’s market as a management cloud SaaS vendor. Adhering to the core values of “Acting in all Conscience, with Integrity and Righteousness”,
the Company is committed to helping businesses achieve their growth targets through dedicated services. It strives to provide clients with the most trusted
enterprise service platform.

According to IDC, Kingdee ranks the first in the China’s market of SaaS ERM (Enterprise Resource Management Cloud Service) and maintains the greatest
market share in the fields of SaaS EA (Enterprise Application Software Cloud Services) and Finance Cloud. Meanwhile, Kingdee is the only China-based
player that remains among the world’s top ten High Productivity aPaaS Vendors rated by Gartner for 2021 and ranks the first in this field in China’s market,
and the only China-based vendor that is included in IDC’s Asia/Pacific Manufacturing ERP SaaS MarketScape for 2021, as a Contender. Further, Kingdee is
the only SaaS service vendor recognized as a leader in both industry empowerment value and technology service capability in the CAICT’s “Double Curve and
Double Quadrant for Enterprise Digital Transformation Development (2022 Edition)”. Attaching great importance to user privacy and data security, Kingdee
has obtained the “grand slam” of security and privacy certifications, including ISO27001, ISO27701, Grade III Protection of Information Security, Cloud
Security Alliance CSA-STAR.

Kingdee’s major cloud service products have been selected by industry lighthouse customers, including Kingdee Cloud Cosmic (new-generation packageable
enterprise-grade PaaS platform), Kingdee Cloud Constellation (large enterprise SaaS management solution), Kingdee Cloud Galaxy (high-growth enterprise
SaaS management solution), Kingdee Cloud Stellar (small and micro enterprise SaaS management solution). Kingdee has provided management software
and cloud services for more than 7.4 million enterprises, governments and organizations across the world.

金蝶國際軟件集團有限公司（
            「金蝶國際」或「金蝶」
                      ）始創於1993年，是香港聯交所主板上市公司（股票代碼：0268.HK），總部位於
中國深圳，是全球領先、中國第一的企業管理雲SaaS公司。以「致良知、走正道、行王道」為核心價值觀，以「全心全意為企業
服務，讓陽光照進每一個企業」為使命，致力成為「最值得託付的企業服務平台」。

IDC數據顯示，金蝶國際在中國SaaS ERM（企業資源管理雲服務）市場排名第一，以及在SaaS EA（企業級應用軟件雲服務）、財務
雲市場佔有率維持排名第一。同時，金蝶成為唯一躋身Gartner 2021年高生產力aPaaS平台全球前十的中國廠商，獲中國第一；
唯一入選IDC 2021亞太區製造ERP SaaS競爭力象限的中國廠商，位居挑戰者(Contenders)象限；此外，在中國信通院《企業數字化
轉型發展雙曲線和雙象限（2022版）    》中，金蝶在行業賦能價值和技術服務能力雙領域中，是唯一被認定為引領者的SaaS服務廠
商。金蝶高度重視使用者隱私和數據安全，已完成產品安全及隱私體系「大滿貫」，包括取得了ISO27001、ISO27701、國家信息
安全等級保護三級認證、國際雲安全聯盟CSA-STAR等多項安全認證。

金蝶旗下的多款雲服務產品獲得標桿企業的青睞，包括金蝶雲 • 蒼穹   （新一代可組裝企業級PaaS平台）
                                                   、金蝶雲 • 星瀚
                                                           （大型企業
SaaS管理雲）
       、金蝶雲 • 星空             、金蝶雲 星辰
               （高成長型企業SaaS管理雲）   •  （小微企業SaaS管理雲）
                                                等，已為世界範圍內超過740萬家
企業、政府等組織提供數字化管理解決方案。

CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告




      Over the past thirty years, Kingdee has gradually developed into
      a global leading and China’s No.1 enterprise management cloud
      SaaS company, supporting Chinese enterprises to become world-
      class. Kingdee will be committed to the Philosophy of “Customer-
      centered, Long-term Adherence to Professionalism; Strivers-
      oriented, Long-term Adherence to Pure and Visionary Mind.”
      Kingdee will continue to insist on better product, better ecosystem,
      better implementation, better service and better reputation. Kingdee
      will bring digitalization to China enterprises to support their high-
      quality development and establish the strong driver of digital
      economy.

      過去30年間，金蝶逐步成長為全球領先、中國第一的
      企業管理雲SaaS公司，助力中國企業加速邁向世界一
      流。我們將繼續堅持「以客戶為中心，長期堅持專業
      主義；以奮鬥者為本，長期堅持明心淨心」的金蝶哲
      學，繼續堅持更好的產品、更好的生態、更好的交
      付、更好的服務、更好的口碑，以數字化推動中國企
      業高質量發展，成為建設數字經濟的強國動能！

     Cash flow interest rate risk                                                                  現金流利率風險

     The Group’s exposure to changes in interest rates is mainly attributable to its               本集團對利率變動的風險敞口主要來自借款，
     borrowings, details of which have been disclosed in Note 22. Borrowings carried               其詳情已在附註22中披露。以浮動利率計息的
     at floating rates exposed the Group to cash flow interest-rate risk whereas those             借款使本集團面臨現金流利率風險，而以固定
     carried at fixed rates exposed the Group to fair value interest-rate risk.                    利率計息的借款使本集團面臨公允價值利率風
                                                                                                   險。

     The Group currently does not use any interest rate swaps to hedge its exposure to             該集團目前不使用任何利率掉期來對沖其利率
     interest rate risk. However, the Group will consider hedging significant interest rate        風險敞口。不過，如果有需要，該集團將考慮
     exposure should the need arise.                                                               對沖巨大的利率風險敞口。

     As at 31 December 2022, bank borrowings of the Group which were bearing at                     在二零二二年十二月三十一日，本集團以浮

     Credit risk                                                                                 信貸風險

     Credit risk arises from cash and cash equivalents, contractual cash flows of debt           信用風險源於現金及現金等價物、以攤餘成本
     instruments carried at amortised cost, at fair value through profit or loss (FVPL)          計量的債務工具合同現金流量、以公允價值計
     and deposits with banks and financial institutions, as well as credit exposures to          量且其變動計入損益的債務工具合同現金流
     customers, including outstanding receivables.                                               量、銀行和其他金融機構存款以及未收應收款
                                                                                                 在內的客戶信用風險。

     The management manages the credit risk of cash and cash equivalents, pledged                為管理來自現金及現金等價物、抵押存款、短
     and short-term and long-term bank deposits and wealth management products                   期銀行存款、長期銀行存款及資產管理產品（歸
     (classified as financial assets at FVPL) by transacting with state-owned financial          類為以公允價值計量且其變動計入損益的金融
     institutions and reputable commercial banks which are all high-credit-quality               資產）的信用風險，本集團管理層僅與中國國有
     financial institutions in mainland China and Hong Kong.                                     或信譽良好的中國大陸及香港的財務機構進行
                                                                                                 交易。

     In relation to trade receivables and contract assets, the Group has two kinds of            對於應收賬款及合同資產，本集團有兩種銷售
     distribution channels, one is sales to distributors and the other is sales to end           管道，一種是銷售給經銷商，另一種是銷售給
     customers.                                                                                  最終客戶。

     distributors, taking into account their financial position, past experience and             品質會考慮其財務狀況、信用歷史記錄及其他
     other factors. Individual risk limits are set based on internal or external ratings in      因素。根據評估的信用品質設定相應的信用額
     accordance with limits set by management. The compliance with credit limits by              度。管理層亦採取一定的監控程序確保經銷商
     distributors is regularly monitored by management.                                          在信用額度內採購。

     For sales to end customers, the Group has no significant concentration of credit            對於銷售給最終客戶，因應收賬款非常零星且
     risk in trade receivables since the balance of trade receivables is composed of             分佈於大量的消費者中，本集團於應收賬款並
     numerous individual small items and the exposure spreads over a large number of             無集中的信貸風險。

Corporate Governance Practices                                                                企業管治常規

The Group consistently promotes good corporate governance, pursuing the five principles       本集團一直以來致力於維持良好的企業管治，奉行確
of ensuring the rights and interests of shareholders, abiding by the directives of the        保股東權益，恪守董事會和管理層職責，履行全面披
Board and management, promoting full disclosure and transparency, ensuring the equal          露與提高透明度之責任，平等對待相關利益者，實施
treatment of stakeholders, and strengthening internal controls and supervision. The           有效的內控與監督的五大原則，努力提升企業價值，
Company endeavors to enhance its enterprise value, so as to ensure the Company’s long-        確保本公司保持長期穩定的發展，保障股東的利益。
term and stable development and safeguard the interests of its shareholders. The details of   下文詳細闡述本公司企業管治常規。
the corporate governance practices of the Company are set out below.

The Company has applied and complied with all the code provisions of the Corporate            截至二零二二年十二月三十一日止年度，除上市規
Governance Code (the “Code”) as set out in Appendix 14 to the Listing Rules throughout        則附錄14所載之企業管治守則（  「守則」

The major roles and functions of the Audit Committee are as follows:                         審核委員會之職責及功能主要包括：

(1)    making recommendations to the Board on the appointment, re-appointment                (1)   就外聘核數師的委任、重新委任及罷免向董事
       and removal of the external auditor, approving the remuneration and terms of                會提供建議、審批外聘核數師的薪酬及聘用條

       or dismissal of the auditors, reviewing and monitoring the external auditor’s               數師的問題；按適用的標準檢討及監察外聘核
       independence, the objectivity and the effectiveness of the audit process in                 數師是否獨立客觀及審計程式是否有效；審核
       accordance with applicable standards. The Audit Committee shall discuss with the            委員會應於審計工作開始前先與外聘核數師討
       external auditor the nature and scope of the audit and reporting obligations before         論核數性質及範疇及有關申報責任，並作為關

2.1   Risk Management Procedure                                                               2.1   風險管理流程

      The Company has designed its major risk management criteria, including risk                   本公司設計了風險識別、風險評估、風險應
      identification, risk assessment, risk response and risk control and reporting:                對、風險監控與報告主要風險管理環節：

      •      Risk identification: The Audit Committee reviews the effectiveness of the              •   風險識別：審核委員會每年檢討本集團
             Group’s internal control and risk management systems each year and will                    內部監控及風險管理系統成效，督促各
             urge all business units and functional departments to identify the internal                業務單元與職能部門識別營運過程中可
             and external risks that may exist during the course of operation;                          能存在的內外部風險；

      •      Risk assessment: All business units and functional departments must assess             •   風險評估：各業務單元與職能部門對識
             the possibility of the occurrence of identified risks and the extent of their              別的風險，從發生的可能性及影響程度
             impact and screen out major risks;                                                         進行評價，篩選出重大風險；


                                                                                               二零二二年年報       金蝶國際軟件集團有限公司   75
CORPORATE GOVERNANCE REPORT
企業管治報告


      •      Risk response: The risk responsibility department properly uses risk                    •   風險應對：風險責任部門對已識別的重
             avoidance, reduction, sharing or tolerance measures to draw up a risk                       大風險，結合集團風險承受度，妥善使
             response scheme according to identified major risks and the Group’s risk                    用風險迴避、降低、分擔或承受等方法
             tolerance, so that the Group can reasonably allocate resources for risk                     制定風險應對方案，以促使集團合理調
             response and reduce its overall risk level to an acceptable level;                          配資源應對風險，使集團的整體風險水
                                                                                                         準降至可接受範圍；

      •      Risk control and reporting: The Group’s risks are controlled and reported               •   風險監控與報告：綜合利用風險預警指
             through the comprehensive use of risk pre-warning indicators, internal                      標、內部審計、定期風險總結報告等形
             audits and regular risk summary reports.                                                    式，對集團風險進行監控與報告。

2.2   Internal Control                                                                         2.2   內部監控

      The Company has designed an internal control system according to the Group’s                   本公司結合本集團業務管理特色，設計了內部
      business management features and has established an internal audit department                  監控系統，並設立內審部門負責內部監控工
      that is responsible for internal controls, conducting risk-oriented internal control           作，每年針對日常資金管理、財務報告管理、
      evaluations with respect to daily fund management, financial report management,                採購、資產管理、業務營運及收款流程等進行
      purchase and asset management, business operations and the flow of income on                   了以風險為導向的內控評價，並定期跟進內控

this regard.                                                                                      們沒有任何報告。

financial statements that are free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error.     負責。

In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the directors are responsible for             在擬備綜合財務報表時，董事負責評估貴公司持續經
assessing the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern, disclosing, as applicable,          營的能力，並在適用情況下披露與持續經營有關的
matters related to going concern and using the going concern basis of accounting unless           事項，以及使用持續經營為會計基礎，除非董事有意
the directors either intend to liquidate the Group or to cease operations, or have no realistic   將貴公司清盤或停止經營，或別無其他實際的替代方
alternative but to do so.                                                                         案。

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the consolidated financial     我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存在由於

is a high level of assurance, but is not a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance     或承擔任何責任。合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能
with ISAs will always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can         保證按照《國際審計準則》進行的審計，在某一重大錯
arise from fraud or error and are considered material if, individually or in the aggregate,    誤陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤
they could reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken on        引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或滙總起來可能影響綜
the basis of these consolidated financial statements.                                          合財務報表使用者依賴綜合財務報表所作出的經濟決

2     Summary of significant accounting policies                                                 2     重要會計政策摘要（續）
      (Continued)

2.2   Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                                          2.2   合併與權益會計原則

2.2.1 Subsidiaries                                                                               2.2.1 子公司

      Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured entities) over which the Group has           子公司為本集團所控制的實體（包括結構化實
      control. The Group controls an entity where the Group is exposed to, or has rights               體）。當本集團從或有權從與該實體的參與中獲
      to, variable returns from its involvement with the entity and has the ability to affect          得變動收益並有能力對這些收益產生影響時，
      those returns through its power to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries are         則其控制該子公司。自控制權轉移至本集團之
      fully consolidated from the date on which control is transferred to the Group. They              日起，子公司被合併。其自喪失實際控制權之
      are deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                                            日起停止合併。

      The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for business                             本集團的業務合併採用會計購買法核算（見附註
      combinations by the Group (refer to Note 2.3).                                                   2.3）
                                                                                                          。

      Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised gains on transactions                        集團內公司之間的交易、結餘及交易的未變現
      between group companies are eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated                    利得予以對銷。未變現損失亦予以對銷，除非
      unless the transaction provides evidence of an impairment of the transferred asset.              交易提供所轉撥資產的減值證據。子公司報告
      Accounting policies of subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to ensure                  的數額已按需要作出改變，以確保與本集團採
      consistency with the policies adopted by the Group.                                              用的政策符合一致。

      Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of subsidiaries are shown                    子公司業績和股權中的非控股權益分別列示於
      separately in the consolidated income statement, consolidated statement of                       合併利潤表、合併綜合收益表、合併股權變動
      comprehensive income, statement of changes in equity and consolidated statement                  表和合併財務狀況表。
      of financial position respectively.

2.2.2 Associates                                                                                 2.2.2 聯營

      Associates are all entities over which the Group has significant influence but not               聯營公司是集團對其有重大影響但不控制或聯
      control or joint control. This is generally the case where the Group holds between               合控制的所有實體。通常情況下，該集團擁有
      20% and 50% of the voting rights. Investments in associates are accounted for                    20%至50%的投票權。聯營公司的投資在最初
      using the equity method of accounting (see Note 2.2.3 below), after initially being              按成本確認後，以權益會計方法核算（見附註
      recognised at cost.                                                                              2.2.3）
                                                                                                            。

2.2.3 Equity method                                                                              2.2.3 權益法

      Under the equity method of accounting, the investments are initially recognised at               權益法下，投資賬面價值最初以成本確認，之
      cost and adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s share of the post-acquisition              後卻按本集團持有投資對象得份額確認投資利
      profits or losses of the investee in profit or loss, and the Group’s share of                    潤或虧損以及其他綜合收益。從聯營公司收到
      movements in other comprehensive income of the investee in other comprehensive                   或應收的股息確認為投資賬面價值的減少。
      income. Dividends received or receivable from associates are recognised as a
      reduction in the carrying amount of the investment.

      Where the Group’s share of losses in an equity-accounted investment equals                       若本集團在股本投資中所承擔的損失與本集團
      or exceeds its interest in the entity, including any other unsecured long-term                   在該實體（包括任何其他無擔保的長期應收賬
      receivables, the Group does not recognise further losses, unless it has incurred                 款）的權益相等或超過其權益，則本集團不承認
      obligations or made payments on behalf of the other entity.                                      進一步的損失，除非本集團已為該實體承擔了

2.3   Business combinations                                                                   2.3   企業合併

      The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for all business                      不論權益工具或其他資產是否被收購，會計的
      combinations, regardless of whether equity instruments or other assets are                    購買法都被用於所有的企業合併。為收購附屬
      acquired. The consideration transferred for the acquisition of a subsidiary                   公司而轉讓的代價包括：
      comprises the:

      •      fair values of the assets transferred                                                  •   轉讓資產的公允價值

      •      liabilities incurred to the former owners of the acquired business                     •   對被收購企業的前所有者承擔的責任

      •      equity interests issued by the Group                                                   •   集團發行的權益

      •      fair value of any asset or liability resulting from a contingent consideration         •   或有對價安排產生的任何資產或負債的
             arrangement, and                                                                           公允價值，以及

      •      fair value of any pre-existing equity interest in the subsidiary.                      •   子公司現有權益的公允價值

      Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and contingent liabilities assumed in a          企業合併中取得的可辨認資產、負債和或有負
      business combination are, with limited exceptions, measured initially at their fair           債除個別情況外，最初按取得日的公允價值計
      values at the acquisition date. The Group recognises any non-controlling interest             量。本集團按按公允價值或按非控股權益在被
      in the acquired entity on an acquisition-by-acquisition basis either at fair value            收購實體的可辨認淨資產中所佔的比例，按逐
      or at the non-controlling interest’s proportionate share of the acquired entity’s net         筆收購的方式確認其在被收購實體中的任何非
      identifiable assets.                                                                          控股權益。

      Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.                                           購買相關成本在產生時支銷。

      The excess of the:                                                                            過量的：

      •      consideration transferred                                                              •   支付的對價

      •      amount of any non-controlling interest in the acquired entity, and                     •   收購實體中任何非控制權益的金額，以
                                                                                                        及

      •      acquisition-date fair value of any previous equity interest in the acquired            •   被收購實體之前任何股權的收購日公允
             entity                                                                                     價值

      over the fair value of the net identifiable assets acquired is recorded as goodwill.          超過公允價值的淨可辨認資產收購記為商譽。

      bargain purchase.                                                                             損益。

                                                                                                     記賬。投資性物業初始按成本列賬，包括相關

      or changes in circumstances indicate that they might be impaired. Other assets are            表明它們可能已減值，則更頻繁地進行測試。
      tested for impairment whenever events or changes in circumstances indicate that               當事件或情況變化表明賬面價值可能無法收回
      the carrying amount may not be recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised for              時，會對其他資產進行減值測試。對於資產的
      the amount by which the asset’s carrying amount exceeds its recoverable amount.               賬面價值超過其可收回金額的金額確認減值損
      The recoverable amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less costs of disposal          失。可收回金額是資產的公允價值減去處置費
      and value in use. For the purposes of assessing impairment, assets are grouped                用和使用價值中的較高者。為了評估減值，將
      at the lowest levels for which there are separately identifiable cash inflows which           資產分類為具有可單獨識別的現金流入的最低
      are largely independent of the cash inflows from other assets or groups of assets             級別，這些現金流入在很大程度上獨立於來
      (cash-generating units). Non-financial assets other than goodwill that suffered an            自其他資產或資產組（現金產生單位）的現金流
      impairment are reviewed for possible reversal of the impairment at the end of each            入。除商譽外，已減值的非金融資產會在每個
      reporting period.                                                                             報告期末進行減值準備的轉回。

      the counterparty.                                                                             破產時，這也必須具有約束力。

      is expected in one year or less (or in the normal operating cycle of the business             應收款的收回預期在一年或以內（如仍在正常經
      if longer), they are classified as current assets. If not, they are presented as non-         營週期中，則可較長時間），其被分類為流動資

      income statement over the period of the borrowings using the effective interest                 差額利用實際利率法於借款期間內在損益表確

      be drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred until the draw-down occurs. To                 用。在此情況下，費用遞延至貸款提取為止。
      the extent there is no evidence that it is probable that some or all of the facility will       如沒有證據證明部份或全部融資將會很可能被
      be drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a pre-payment for liquidity services and               提取，則該費用資本化作為流動資金服務的預
      amortised over the period of the facility to which it relates.                                  付款，並按有關的融資期間攤銷。

      when the obligation specified in the contract is discharged, cancelled or expired.              款自財務狀況表中刪除。已消除或轉撥至另一
      The difference between the carrying amount of a financial liability that has been               方的金融負債的賬面值與已付對價的差額，包

      take a substantial period of time to get ready for their intended use or sale, are              資產）的借款成本，加入該等資產的成本內，直

2.21 Current and deferred income tax                                                              2.21 當期及遞延所得稅

      period’s taxable income based on the applicable income tax rate for each                          轄區的適用所得稅率，根據當期應納稅所得額
      jurisdiction adjusted by changes in deferred tax assets and liabilities attributable to           應納稅所得額，該稅率由因暫時性差異和未使
      temporary differences and to unused tax losses.                                                   用的稅損引起的遞延所得稅資產和負債的變化

(a)   Current income tax                                                                          (a)   當期所得稅

      The current income tax charge is calculated on the basis of the tax laws enacted                  當期所得稅費用是根據在公司及其子公司經營
      or substantively enacted at the statement of financial position date in the countries             並產生應納稅所得額的國家在財務狀況表日期
      where the Company and its subsidiaries operate and generate taxable income.                       已頒布或實質上已頒布的稅法計算的。管理層
      Management periodically evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect                     會就適用稅法受到解釋的情況定期評估納稅申
      to situations in which applicable tax regulation is subject to interpretation and                 報表中的頭寸，並考慮稅務機關是否有可能接
      considers whether it is probable that a taxation authority will accept an uncertain               受不確定的稅收待遇。本集團根據最可能的金
      tax treatment. The Group measures its tax balances either based on the most likely                額或預期價值計量其稅收餘額，具體取決於哪
      amount or the expected value, depending on which method provides a better                         種方法可以更好地預測不確定性的解決方法。
      prediction of the resolution of the uncertainty.

      differences arising between the tax bases of assets and liabilities and their carrying            基礎與其在合併財務報表中的賬面價值之間產

      are not recognised if they arise from the initial recognition of goodwill, the deferred           項負債是由於初始確認商譽而產生的，則不予
      income tax is not accounted for if it arises from initial recognition of an asset or              確認；如果遞延所得稅是由於在企業合併以外
      liability in a transaction other than a business combination that at the time of the              的交易中對資產或負債的初始確認而產生的，
      transaction affects neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss. Deferred income                則不計入遞延所得稅。該交易既不影響會計也
      tax is determined using tax rates (and laws) that have been enacted or substantially              不影響應稅損益。遞延所得稅是使用在財務狀
      enacted by the statement of financial position date and are expected to apply when                況表日期已經制定或大致制定的稅率（和法律）

2.24 Provisions                                                                                 2.24 準備

      Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present legal or constructive                    當本集團因已發生的事件而產生現有的法律或
      obligation as a result of past events; it is probable that an outflow of resources will         推定義務﹔很可能需要有資源的流出以結算義
      be required to settle the obligation; and the amount has been reliably estimated.               務﹔及金額已被可靠估計時，當作出準備。準
      Provisions are not recognised for future operating losses.                                      備不會就未來經營虧損確認準備。

      Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood that an outflow will            如有多項類似義務，其需要在結算中有資源流
      be required in settlement is determined by considering the class of obligations as a            出的可能性，則可根據義務的類別整體考慮。
      whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of an outflow with respect to           即使在同一義務類別所包含的任何一個項目相
      any one item included in the same class of obligations may be small.                            關的資源流出的可能性極低，仍須確認準備。

      Provisions are measured at the present value of management’s best estimate of                   撥備按管理層於報告期末償還現時義務所需開
      the expenditure required to settle the present obligation at the end of the reporting           支的最佳估計的現值計量。用於確定現值的折
      period. The discount rate used to determine the present value is a pre-tax rate                 現率是稅前利率，其反映了貨幣時間價值和負
      that reflects current market assessments of the time value of money and the risks               債特定風險的當前市場評估。隨著時間的流
      specific to the liability. The increase in the provision due to the passage of time is          逝，準備金的增加被確認為利息支出。
      recognised as interest expense.

      of the Company, excluding any costs of servicing equity other than ordinary                   利潤，不包括除普通股以外的任何股本服務成
      shares by the weighted average number of ordinary shares outstanding during the               本，按本財政年度已發行普通股的加權平均數
      financial year, adjusted for bonus elements in ordinary shares issued during the              量計算，並根據當年發行的普通股中的獎金因
      year and excluding treasury shares.                                                           素進行調整，不包括庫存股。

      earnings per share to take into account:                                                      位，以顧及：

             with dilutive potential ordinary shares, and                                               潛在普通股相關；

             been outstanding assuming the conversion of all dilutive potential ordinary                所有稀釋性潛在普通股的轉換。
             shares.

2.27 Interest income                                                                          2.27 利息收益

      Interest income from financial assets at FVPL calculated using the effective interest         依有效利息方式計算的以公允價值計量且其變
      method is recognised in the income statement as realised gains on financial assets            動計入損益的金融資產利息收入已在損益表中
      at FVPL within “Other gains/losses – net”.                                                    確認為包含在「其他虧損╱利得－淨額」的淨值
                                                                                                    中以公允價值計量且其變動計入損益的金融資
                                                                                                    產的實現利得。

      Interest income on financial assets at amortised cost calculated using the effective          使用實際利率法計算的以攤餘成本計量的金融
      interest method is recognised in profit or loss as part of other income.                      資產的利息收入在損益中確認為其他收入的一
                                                                                                    部分。

      Interest income is presented as finance income where it is earned from financial              利息收入是從為現金管理目的而持有的金融資
      assets that are held for cash management purposes, see Note 30 below. Any other               產中賺取的財務收入，見下文附註30。任何其
      interest income is included in other income.                                                  他利息收入均計入其他收益。

      Interest income is calculated by applying the effective interest rate to the gross            利息收入是通過對金融資產的賬面總額應用實
      carrying amount of a financial asset except for financial assets that subsequently            際利率來計算的，但隨後發生信貸減損的金融
      become credit-impaired. For credit-impaired financial assets the effective interest           資產除外。對於信用減損的金融資產，實際利
      rate is applied to the net carrying amount of the financial asset (after deduction of         率適用於該金融資產的賬面淨額（扣除損失準備
      the loss allowance).                                                                          金後）

2.28 Leases                                                                                   2.28 租賃

     Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a corresponding liability at the           租賃在本集團可供租賃的資產使用當日確認為
     date at which the leased asset is available for use by the Group.                            使用權資產和相應的負債。

     Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease components. The Group allocates               合同可能同時包含租賃和非租賃組成部分。本
     the consideration in the contract to the lease and non-lease components based on             集團根據相對獨立價格將合同中的對價分配給
     their relative stand-alone prices. However, for leases of real estate for which the          租賃和非租賃部分。然而，對於本集團為承租
     Group is a lessee, it has elected not to separate lease and non-lease components             人的房地產租賃，它選擇不將租賃和非租賃組
     and instead accounts for these as a single lease component.                                  成部分分開，而是將其作為一個單獨的租賃組
                                                                                                  成部分進行會計處理。

     Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and contain a wide range of                租賃條款是根據個人情況協商確定的，其中包
     different terms and conditions. The lease agreements do not impose any covenants             含各種不同的條款和條件。除了出租人持有的
     other than the security interests in the leased assets that are held by the lessor.          租賃資產中的擔保權益外，租賃協議不施加任
     Leased assets may not be used as security for borrowing purposes.                            何其他約定。租賃資產不得用作借貸擔保。

     Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially measured on a present              租賃產生的資產和負債按現值進行初始計量。
     value basis. Lease liabilities include the net present value of the following lease          租賃負債包括以下租賃付款額的淨現值：
     payments:

     •      fixed payments (including in-substance fixed payments), less any lease                •     固定付款額（包括實質固定付款額），扣除
            incentives receivable;                                                                      任何應收的租賃激勵；

     •      variable lease payment that are based on an index or a rate, initially                •     基於指數或比率確定的可變租賃付款
            measured using the index or rate as at the commencement date;                               額，採用租賃期開始日的指數或比率進
                                                                                                        行初始計量；

     •      amounts expected to be payable by the Group under residual value                      •     本集團根據餘值擔保預計應付的金額；
            guarantees;

     •      the exercise price of a purchase option if the Group is reasonably certain to         •     本集團合理確定將行使的購買選擇權的
            exercise that option, and                                                                   行權價格

     •      payments of penalties for terminating the lease, if the lease term reflects the       •     以及在租賃期反映出本集團將行使選擇
            Group exercising that option.                                                               權的情況下終止租賃的罰款金額。

     Lease payments to be made under reasonably certain extension options are also                在合理的某些延期選擇權下支付的租賃付款也
     included in the measurement of the liability.                                                包括在負債的計量中。

     The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate implicit in the lease. If          租賃付款使用租賃中隱含的利率折現。如果
     that rate cannot be readily determined, which is generally the case for leases in            無法輕易確定該利率（本集團中的租賃通常如
     the Group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used, being the rate that the          此），則使用承租人的增量借款利率，即單個承
     individual lessee would have to pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an asset         租人為獲得具有類似價值的資產所必需的資金
     of similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic environment with            而必須藉入的利率在類似的經濟環境中，具有
     similar terms, security and conditions.                                                      相似的條款，安全性和條件的使用權資產。

      with all attached conditions.                                                               助按其公允價值確認入賬。

      the related assets.

       is no material credit risk inherent in the Group’s outstanding balances of other                本集團其他應收款項的未清償餘額不存在重大
       receivables.                                                                                    的內在信用風險。

       Trade receivables and contract assets                                                          應收賬款和合同資產

       The Group applies the IFRS 9 simplified approach to measuring expected credit                  本集團採用國際財務報告準則第9號的簡化方法
       losses which uses a lifetime expected loss allowance for all trade receivables and             計量預期信用損失，即對所有應收賬款及合同
       contract assets.                                                                               資產確認整個存續期的預期損失撥備。

       To measure the expected credit losses, trade receivables and contract assets have              為了衡量預期的信用損失，已根據共同的信用
       been grouped based on shared credit risk characteristics and the invoice days. The             風險特徵和賬齡對應收賬款和合同資產進行了
       contract assets relate to unbilled work in progress and have substantially the same            分組。合同資產與未開票的在建工程有關，並
       risk characteristics as the trade receivables within aging bucket for the same types           且具有與相同類型合同應收賬款實質上相同的
       of contracts. The Group has therefore concluded that the expected loss rates for               風險特徵。因此，小組得出結論，應收賬款的
       trade receivables are a reasonable approximation of the loss rates for the contract            預期損失率是合同資產損失率的合理近似值。
       assets.

       The expected loss rates are estimated base on the roll rate approach considering               預期損失率是基於滾動利率法，考慮過往歷史
       the invoice days of trade receivables across historical years and the corresponding            年度的貿易應收款項和合同資產的發票天數以
       historical credit losses experienced within the period. The historical loss rates are          及該期間的相應歷史信用損失而估計的。調整
       adjusted to reflect current and forward-looking information on macroeconomic                   歷史損失率，以反映有關影響客戶清算應收款
       factors affecting the ability of the customers to settle the receivables. The Group            能力的宏觀經濟因素的當前和前瞻性資訊。本
       has identified production price index, broad measure of money supply (M2), and                 集團已將生產價格指數、廣義貨幣供應量、固
       fixed asset investment price index as the main influencing factors, and accordingly            定資產投資價格指數識別為主要影響因素，並
       adjusts the historical loss rates based on expected changes in these factors. The              根據其預期變化對歷史損失率進行調整。本集
       Group would also monitor the counterparties individually and identify specific                 團亦會監察交易方，並識別具有不同特徵的特
       counterparties with distinct characteristics for individual impairment assessment.             定交易方進行個別減值評估。

3.3   Capital risk management                                                                3.3   資金風險管理

      The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard the Group’s ability            本集團的資金管理政策，是保障集團能持續運
      to continue as a going concern in order to provide returns for owners and benefits           營，以為股東提供回報和為其他權利持有人提
      for other stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital structure to reduce the            供利益，同時維持最佳的資本結構以降低資金
      cost of capital.                                                                             成本。

      In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust the               為了維持或調整資本結構，本集團可能會調整
      amount of dividends paid to owners, return capital to shareholders, issue new                支付予股東的股利數額、向股東退還資本、發
      shares or sell assets to reduce debt.                                                        行新股或出售資產以減低債務。

      Consistent with others in the industry, the Group monitors capital on the basis              與業內其他公司一樣，本集團利用資本負債比
      of the gearing ratio. This ratio is calculated as net debt divided by total capital.         率監察其資本。此比率按照債務淨額除以總資
      Net debt is calculated as total borrowings (including “current and non-current               本計算。債務淨額為總借款（包括合併財務狀況
      borrowings” as shown in the consolidated statement of financial position) less               表所列的「流動及非流動借款」
                                                                                                                ）減去現金和現金
      cash and cash equivalents. Total capital is calculated as “equity” as shown in the           等價物。總資本為「權益」
                                                                                                              （如合併財務狀況表所
      consolidated statement of financial position plus net debt.                                  列）
                                                                                                    加債務淨額。

      The gearing ratios at 31 December 2022 and 2021 were as follows:                             在二零二二年及二零二一年十二月三十一日，

      on the financial budgets approved by management. The goodwill impairment                       率和息稅折舊及攤銷前利潤(「EBITDA」)利潤率、

      is required in determining the provision for income taxes. There are many                      定全球所得稅準備時，需要作出重大判斷。有

      The Group recognises liabilities for anticipated tax audit issues based on estimates           確定的。本集團根據對是否需要繳付額外稅款
      of whether additional taxes will be due. Where the final tax outcome of these                  的估計，就預期稅務審計項目確認負債。如此

      such determination is made.                                                                    稅和遞延所得稅資產和負債。

      experience of the existing products and the prospects of the markets. Any severe               未來經濟利益。任何涉及市場表現及科技發展

      the development costs capitalised.

      withholding tax that would be payable on the unremitted earnings of approximately              4,307,076,000元

      payable monthly. Lease payments for some contracts include CPI increases, but                 月支付。一些合同的租金包括消費者物價指數
      there are no other variable lease payments that depend on an index or rate. Where             增長，但沒有其他依賴於指數或費率的可變租
      considered necessary to reduce credit risk, the Group required the tenants to                 金。本集團認為有必要降低信用風險時，要求
      provide deposits for the term of lease contract.                                              租戶在租賃合同期內提供保證金。

      Although the Group is exposed to changes in the residual value at the end of the              雖然本集團在當前租賃期結束時，會面臨剩餘

      will not immediately realise any reduction in residual value at the end of these              賃期，因此，在這些租賃期結束時，本集團不
      leases. Expectations about the future residual values are reflected in the fair value         會立即意識到剩餘價值的任何減少。對未來剩
      of the properties.                                                                            餘價值的預期反映在房地產的公允價值中。

      to Note 37.

      Valuation processes of the Group                                                              本集團的估值流程

      The Group’s finance department includes a team that reviews the valuations                    本集團財務部設有一個小組，專責就財務報告

      processes and results are held among the CFO, the valuation team and the                      期和年度報告日期，CFO、估值小組與估值師
      valuers at least once every six months, in line with the Group’s interim and annual           最少每六個月開會一次，討論估值流程和相關
      reporting dates.                                                                              結果。

      Valuation techniques                                                                                            估值技術

      For completed office buildings, the valuation was determined using income                                       已落成辦公大樓的估值利用收益法，按重大不
      approach based on significant unobservable inputs. These inputs include:                                        可觀察輸入釐定。這些輸入包括：


                                                                                                              Range of                          Relationship of unobservable
      Unobservable inputs    Description                                                                      unobservable inputs               inputs to fair value
                                                                                                              不可觀察輸入的                           不可觀察輸入
      不可觀察輸入                 描述                                                                               範圍                                對公允價值的關係


      Term yields            Based on yields extracting from terms of current leases                          4.75% (2021: 5.00%)The higher the term yields rate, the lower the

             virtue of an agreement. Consequently, the Group consolidates Kingdee Apusic                     取得超過半數投票權。因此，本集團將深圳
             and its wholly-owned subsidiaries, Beijing Kingdee Middleware Software System                   天燕，以及它的全資子公司北京金蝶天燕雲

     (b)     The Group does not have legal ownership in the equity of these operating                  (b)   集團對上述運營實體並無法定所有權。集團
             companies. The Group has entered into several contractual arrangements with                     自這些運營實體成立日起與它們及其股東訂
             them and their equity holders since their incorporation. These contractual                      立若干協定，該等協定不可撤銷且使得集
             arrangements are irrevocable and enable the Group to:                                           團：

             –       exercise effective financial and operational control over these operating               －    對運營實體可行使有效的財務及經營
                     companies;                                                                                   控制權；

                     operating companies;                                                                         決權；

             –       receive substantially all of the economic interest returns generated by                 －    對運營實體進行業務支持從而取得其
                     these operating companies in consideration for the business support;                         運營產生的全部經濟利益；

                     companies from their equity holders as collateral for all the payments                       定下應付集團款項的抵押。
                     due to the Group and as security for performance of obligations under
                     the contractual arrangements.

             As a result of such contractual arrangements, the Group has rights to variable                  根據這些協定的約定，本集團享有可變回
             returns from its involvement with them and has the ability to affect those returns              報，且有能力透過其對這些運營實體（            「結構

             to control them. Consequently, the Group regards them as controlled structured                  制。因此，本集團合併財務報表將它們視為
             entities in the consolidated financial statements.                                              控制結構化主體。

      Company’s shares) for the same total proceeds is added to the denominator as the               的股份數目，與假設購股權行使而應已發行的
      number of ordinary shares issued for no consideration.                                         股份數目作出比較。有關差額將加進分母，作
                                                                                                     為無需代價而發行之普通股。

(i)    Accounting policy choice for non-controlling interests                                     (i)    少數股東的會計政策選擇

       The Group recognised non-controlling interests in an acquired entity either at fair               本集團以公允價值或非控股權益在被收購實體
       value or at the non-controlling interest’s proportionate share of the acquired entity’s           的可辨認淨資產中所佔的比例確認被收購實體
       net identifiable assets. This decision was made on an acquisition-by-acquisition                  的非控制性權益。 該決定是在逐個收購的基礎
       basis. For the non-controlling interests in Yunji, the Group elected to recognise                 上做出的。 對於雲集的非控股權益，本集團選
       the non-controlling interests at its proportionate share of the acquired net                      擇按照其在取得的可辨認淨資產中所佔的比例
       identifiable assets. See Note 2.3 for the Group’s accounting policies for business                確認非控股權益。 本集團企業合併的會計政策
       combinations.                                                                                     見附注 2.3。
